Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that RPMA substantially improves the recovery accuracy of projection matrices and consistently outperforms conventional spectral projection methods for community detection and clustering under noisy environments.

</details>


### [178] [Counterfactual Explainability Framework With CycleGAN And Counterfactual-Classifier Alignnment Score for Retinal Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21068)
*Kritanu Chattopadhyay,Sayanjit Singha Roy,Soumya Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CounterFundus是一个基于CycleGAN的反事实可解释性框架，通过将病理眼底图像转换为视觉上合理的健康对应图像并生成差异图来定位疾病相关视网膜变化，同时引入CCAS指标量化解释空间一致性，并通过反事实增强提升下游分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在眼底疾病检测中表现优异，但其缺乏可解释性，尤其是模型决策未能关联临床上有意义的视网膜区域，阻碍了临床采用。

Method: 采用CycleGAN生成病理到正常的反事实转换，结合EfficientNet-B5分类器，通过差异图定位病灶；提出CCAS（结合Spearman相关、二值IoU和指向精度）量化反事实与分类器显著性图的空间一致性；利用CCAS筛选的反事实进行数据增强。

Result: EigenCAM对齐评估表明反事实解释在CCAS所有维度上与分类器相关视网膜证据空间一致；消融研究证实CCAS过滤的反事实增强可提升下游分类性能。

Conclusion: CounterFundus建立了一个临床可落地的可解释AI框架，通过视觉上合理的疾病到正常视网膜翻译提供反事实解释，为眼底疾病检测提供可信赖的决策依据。

Abstract: Automated detection of vision impairing retina-based ocular conditions from fundus images is important for early screening, timely referral and reducing dependency on specialist-only assessment, for which neural network-based deep learning (DL) models have been widely utilized. However, explainability of the DL frameworks remains a major bottleneck for clinical adoption, particularly when model decisions are not linked to retinal regions that are clinically meaningful. To address this issue, this study presents CounterFundus, a novel CycleGAN-driven counterfactual explainability framework, integrating EfficientNet-B5-based retinal disease detection with visually interpretable disease-to-normal fundus image translation. For each pathological image, the counterfactual yielded by the CycleGAN generator represents an estimated healthy counterpart and the resultant difference map is utilized to localize disease-associated retinal changes. Unlike conventional post-hoc saliency methods, CounterFundus provides counterfactual explanations through visually plausible disease-to-normal retinal translation. Thereafter, to quantify the spatial agreement between counterfactual difference maps and classifier saliency, the Counterfactual-Classifier Alignment Score (CCAS) is introduced, embedding Spearman correlation, binary IoU and pointing accuracy into a single assessment protocol. To this end, EigenCAM-aligned evaluation demonstrates that the generated counterfactual explanations remain spatially consistent with classifier-relevant retinal evidence across all CCAS dimensions. Along with that, ablation studies further confirm that CCAS-filtered counterfactual augmentation improves the downstream classification performance in fundus images, establishing CounterFundus as a clinically-grounded, explainable artificially intelligence (XAI) framework for retinal disease detection.

</details>


### [179] [From Evaluation to Optimisation: Hierarchy-Aware Training Signals for CWE Prediction in Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21069)
*Muntasir Adnan,Manile Srun,Carlos C. N. Kuhn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了ALPHA基准提出的分层惩罚函数可作为训练信号，比较了三种传递机制（监督微调、双头分类损失、基于归一化惩罚密集奖励的强化学习），发现监督方法在分布偏移下甚至低于零样本基线，而GRPO成功将Qwen2.5-Coder-7B的累积ALPHA惩罚降低27.9%，与4.5倍大的零样本教师模型达到统计持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALPHA基准提出了一个分层感知惩罚用于评估CWE级别的漏洞预测，并理论上认为该惩罚也可作为训练信号，本文旨在验证这一理论猜想。

Method: 比较三种传递机制：监督微调、双头分类损失、以及基于归一化惩罚密集奖励的强化学习（GRPO）。

Result: 监督方法在分布偏移下低于零样本基线，而GRPO成功将Qwen2.5-Coder-7B在SVEN数据集上的累积ALPHA惩罚降低27.9%（贪婪解码）和25.5%（采样解码，p=0.005），达到与4.5倍大的零样本教师模型统计持平。

Conclusion: 分层惩罚作为训练信号的价值很大程度上取决于其传递的直接性：监督方式失效，而强化学习的密集奖励机制有效。

Abstract: The original ALPHA benchmark introduced a taxonomy-aware penalty for evaluating CWE-level vulnerability prediction in Python and proposed that the penalty could theoretically also serve as a training signal. This paper provides that validation. We compare three delivery mechanisms: supervised fine-tuning, a dual-head classification loss, and reinforcement learning with a dense reward derived from the normalised penalty. We find that supervised approaches consistently regress below the zero-shot baseline under distribution shift, while GRPO succeeds. Our best policy reduces the cumulative ALPHA penalty of Qwen2.5-Coder-7B on Security Hardening and Adversarial Testing (SVEN) dataset by 27.9% under greedy decoding, and by 25.5% under sampled decoding(p = 0.005, Welch's t-test), reaching statistical parity with its 4.5x larger zero-shot teacher. We conclude that the value of a hierarchical penalty as a training signal depends largely on the directness of its delivery.

</details>


### [180] [Spectral Transformation for Layer-wise Global Rank Discovery in Federated LoRA for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21074)
*Hariharan Ramesh,Jyotikrishna Dass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecTraL方法，通过谱变换和Householder正交变换直接在低秩潜在空间聚合联邦学习中的LoRA适配器，利用随机矩阵理论的尖峰协方差模型自动发现全局最优秩，无需人工调参，同时引入补零感知初始化避免客户端重合并操作，在ViT联邦微调中实现更优的精度-通信权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦LoRA聚合策略存在根本性缺陷：独立平均LoRA因子会引入交叉项聚合误差；保留异构客户端秩的拼接方法增加下载开销且需将全局LoRA合并回预训练权重，导致重初始化延迟和不稳定收敛；其他方案通过重构密集权重更新或训练辅助模型来修正聚合误差，进一步增加服务器端负担。

Method: SpecTraL方法：1) 堆叠客户端的局部LoRA模块，在低秩潜在空间中直接执行正交Householder变换，避免稠密权重重构；2) 基于随机矩阵理论的Spiked Covariance Model，从非独立同分布噪声中解析分离全局共识信号，自动发现最优逐层全局秩；3) 提出补零感知初始化框架，让客户端纳入残差LoRA维度而无需重新合并到预训练基座模型。

Result: 在DomainNet和NICO++数据集上对ViT-B/16和ViT-L/16进行联邦微调实验，展示了改进的精度-通信权衡、降低的服务器计算量以及消除了秩选择的超参数搜索需求。

Conclusion: SpecTraL通过统一的谱变换设计，同时解决了联邦LoRA聚合中的数学不一致性、通信开销、服务器计算负担和超参数调优问题，在保持高效通信的同时实现稳定收敛和良好精度。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Vision Transformers (ViTs) with low-rank adapters (LoRA) promises better communication efficiency under federated setup, yet existing aggregation strategies face fundamental limitations. Independently averaging these LoRA factors is mathematically inconsistent, introducing cross-term aggregation error. In contrast, approaches that preserve heterogeneous client ranks by concatenating local adapters on the server substantially increase download cost and often require merging global LoRA updates into pretrained weights on the clients, causing reinitialization lag and unstable convergence. Other approaches further increase server-side overhead by reconstructing dense weight updates or training auxiliary models to refine aggregation error. In this work, we propose SpecTraL, spectral transformation for layer-wise global rank discovery, that resolves these challenges within a unified design. SpecTraL stacks local LoRA modules from clients and performs orthonormal Householder Transformation of the stacked adapters directly in the low-rank latent space, eliminating dense reconstruction of the global update and any auxiliary refinement on the server. By leveraging the Spiked Covariance Model from Random Matrix Theory, SpecTraL analytically separates the global consensus signal from non-IID noise, discovering optimal layer-wise global ranks without manual hyperparameter tuning. To match local ranks in subsequent rounds, we introduce a padding-aware initialization framework that lets clients incorporate residual LoRA dimensions without re-merging them into the pre-trained base model. Experiments on federated fine-tuning of ViT-B/16 and ViT-L/16 over DomainNet and NICO++ demonstrate improved accuracy-communication trade-offs, reduced server computation, and elimination of hyperparameter search for rank selection. Our code is available at https://github.com/DASS-Lab-Group/SpecTraL

</details>


### [181] [Nipping the Butterfly Effect in the Bud: Self-Output Fine-Tuning for Autoregressive Weather Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21080)
*Yun-Ye Cai,Hsuan-Tien Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自回归深度学习天气预测（DLWP）中长时程预报误差快速增长的现象，发现其根源是输出误差与输入分布偏移之间的反馈循环，并提出了一种简单有效的即插即用策略——自输出微调（SOFT），通过利用模型自身的一步预测来校准第一步推理中出现的偏置输入分布，显著降低了长时程预测误差和分布差异，达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归深度学习天气预测虽具可扩展性和灵活性，但其预测误差在长时程预报中快速增长，这一误差增长现象的深层机制尚未被充分理解，亟需从理论和实证角度进行分析并提出解决方案。

Method: 首先从理论角度分析了自回归过程中输出误差与输入分布偏移之间的反馈循环机制；其次通过实证验证了分布偏移在最早推理阶段（第一步自回归步骤）就已可检测；最后提出Self-Output Fine-Tuning (SOFT)即插即用策略，利用模型自身的一步预测来校准第一步推理中出现的偏置输入分布。

Result: SOFT在长时程预测任务上达到了最先进的性能，显著降低了预测误差和分布差异；实验证明该策略虽简单但有效，能够打破误差增长的反馈循环。

Conclusion: 自输出微调（SOFT）的成功强调了重新审视深度学习天气预测基本流程的重要性，代表了大气科学领域的关键流程进展。

Abstract: Long-horizon weather forecasting is a fundamental challenge in atmospheric science, for which autoregressive Deep Learning Weather Prediction (DLWP) has emerged as the primary paradigm. Although the autoregressive pipeline is highly scalable and flexible, its prediction errors grow rapidly over long forecasting horizons. In this work, we study this error growth phenomenon from both theoretical and empirical perspectives. Our analysis reveals that the growth is driven by a feedback loop between output errors and input distribution shifts. Specifically, the autoregressive process amplifies small initial output errors, which progressively corrupt subsequent input distributions, echoing the butterfly effect in atmospheric science and ultimately deteriorating forecasting accuracy over longer horizons. Furthermore, we show that this distributional shift originates at the earliest stage of inference, with out-of-distribution signatures detectable as early as the first autoregressive step. To mitigate this issue, we propose \textbf{Self-Output Fine-Tuning (SOFT)}, a plug-and-play strategy that leverages the model's own one-step predictions to calibrate the biased input distribution encountered at the first step. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, despite its simplicity, SOFT achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-horizon forecasting tasks and substantially reduces both prediction errors and distributional discrepancy. The success of SOFT highlights the importance of reexamining the fundamental pipeline of deep learning weather prediction, representing a critical pipeline advance for atmospheric science.

</details>


### [182] [CASC: Causal Adversarial Subspace Clustering for Multivariate Spatiotemporal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21088)
*Francis Ndikum Nji,Vandana Janeja,Jianwu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果对抗子空间聚类（CASC）框架，通过集成U-Net对抗架构、FAConvLSTM层和图注意力Transformer自表达网络，结合因果子空间保持损失与动态时间子空间演化损失，将深度子空间聚类从相关性驱动范式转变为因果-时间机制发现框架，以捕捉高维时空数据中的因果依赖、局部空间交互和长程时间动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度子空间聚类方法主要依赖几何自表达性、假设静态子空间结构，未能捕捉复杂时空系统中固有的因果依赖、局部空间相互作用和长程时间动态。

Method: 提出CASC框架，包含：(1) U-Net启发的深度对抗聚类架构与堆叠FAConvLSTM层；(2) 图注意力Transformer自表达网络联合建模局部空间关系、全局依赖和长程时间交互；(3) 因果子空间保持损失对齐自表达系数与潜在因果关系；(4) 动态时间子空间演化损失捕捉非平稳环境中的子空间结构演化与时间机制转换。

Result: 将深度子空间聚类从相关性驱动范式转变为因果-时间机制发现框架。

Conclusion: CASC框架能够有效发现高维时空数据中演化的潜在机制，弥补了现有方法在因果建模和动态子空间结构捕捉方面的不足。

Abstract: Deep subspace clustering plays a critical role in applications involving multivariate spatiotemporal data, such as sea ice monitoring, disease spread analysis, and tracking neuro-degeneration over time. Despite recent advances, existing methods primarily rely on geometric self-expressiveness, assume static subspace structures, and often fail to capture causal dependencies, local spatial interactions, and long-range temporal dynamics inherent in complex spatiotemporal systems. To address these limitations, we propose a novel Causal Adversarial Subspace Clustering (CASC) framework for discovering evolving latent regimes in high-dimensional spatiotemporal data. CASC integrates a U-Net-inspired deep adversarial clustering architecture with stacked FAConvLSTM layers to preserve spatial and temporal structure while learning robust latent representations. A graph attention transformer-based self-expressive network is introduced to jointly model local spatial relationships, global dependencies, and long-range temporal interactions. Furthermore, we propose two new learning objectives: (1) a Causal Subspace Preservation Loss that aligns self-expression coefficients with latent causal relationships, encouraging clusters to reflect underlying causal processes rather than simple feature similarity, and (2) a Dynamic Temporal Subspace Evolution Loss that captures evolving subspace structures and temporal regime transitions in nonstationary environments. Together, these components transform deep subspace clustering from a correlation-driven paradigm into a causal-temporal regime discovery framework.

</details>


### [183] [Training Large Language Models for Self-Explanation Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21090)
*Yeoktatt Cheah,María Pérez-Ortiz,Noah Y. Siegel,Oana-Maria Camburu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种强化学习方法，通过修改现有 faithfulness 指标作为 RL 训练目标，直接优化 LLM 自解释的忠实度。在 Llama3.1-8B 和 Qwen3-8B 上的实验表明，该方法能显著提升 Phi-CCT 指标（从接近 0 到 0.664），并在 OOD 任务上达到 0.691。跨干预泛化表现模型依赖，且通过分析排除了奖励破解行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅关注评估忠实度或使用推理时提示框架来改进自解释的可追踪性，缺乏直接优化模型参数以生成忠实自解释的机制。本文旨在弥补这一空白。

Method: 将现有忠实度指标（Phi-CCT）修改为 RL 训练目标，采用两种干预类型（随机词插入和用户偏见插入），基于每个样本的 Phi-CCT 相关性指标作为奖励信号，对 Llama3.1-8B 和 Qwen3-8B 进行 RL 微调。

Result: 分布内 Phi-CCT 从接近零提升至 0.664，分布外（如 StrategyQA）达 0.691。跨干预泛化方面，仅在随机词插入上训练的 Llama3.1-8B 能非零迁移到用户偏见短语，但反向和 Qwen3-8B 未复现。分析排除了奖励破解行为。

Conclusion: 模型可以通过训练隐式识别并披露影响决策的因素，为减少 LLM 中不忠实推理提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: We propose a Reinforcement Learning (RL) method to directly optimize the faithfulness of self-explanations - the extent to which a model's generated reasoning accurately reflects its internal decision-making process. While existing work focuses on evaluating faithfulness or using inference-time prompting frameworks to improve an LLM's self-explanation's tractability, these approaches do not provide a mechanism to directly optimize a model's parameters to generate faithful self-explanations. We bridge this gap by modifying existing faithfulness metrics into an RL training objective. We investigate (1) if models can be trained to accurately detect factors that affect their decisions, and (2) whether RL can directly optimize for the disclosure of these factors thereby improving LLM self-explanations' faithfulness. We experiment with two intervention types: random-word insertions and user-bias insertions, using a per-sample reward derived from the Phi-CCT correlation metric. RL fine-tuned Llama3.1-8B and Qwen3-8B show substantial improvements on the Phi-CCT faithfulness metric, with in-distribution scores rising from near-zero to as high as 0.664, and out-of-distribution scores reaching up to 0.691 on held-out tasks such as StrategyQA. Cross-intervention generalization is weaker but more interesting: a priori we would not expect a model trained only on random word insertions to generalize to user-bias phrases, yet Llama3.1-8B shows non-zero transfer in this direction. The reverse direction and Qwen3-8B do not replicate this, indicating model-dependent and setup-dependent effects we cannot yet explain. Lastly we analyze model behavior to rule out reward gaming behaviors that often plague RL training. Ultimately, we show that models can be trained to implicitly identify influential factors and disclose them, offering a scalable path toward reducing unfaithful reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [184] [A Polynomial Architecture-Attribution Co-Design Framework for Exact Aumann-Shapley Attribution in GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21094)
*Bizu Feng,Zhimu Yang,Shuming Wang,Shaode Yu,Yuan Cheng,Xiaojun Qian,Zixin Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出APEX框架，通过模型-归因协同设计，在多项式GNN架构下实现Aumann-Shapley归因积分的精确计算，利用Gauss-Legendre求积以最少评估点获得精确到浮点精度的归因结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径积分归因方法（如Integrated Gradients）在深度GNN中依赖有限样本数值近似，存在求积误差与计算成本之间的权衡问题，缺乏精确可计算的归因方案。

Method: 提出APEX框架，核心是PolyGIN网络——一种保留有界多元多项式形式的GIN式图网络。通过模型-归因协同设计，使得标量模型分数（如logits）保持多项式形式，从而利用Gauss-Legendre求积在2^{L-1}个确定评估点上精确计算Aumann-Shapley路径积分。

Result: PolyGIN在合成和真实图基准上保持有竞争力的预测性能；APEX框架相比基线方法实现了更高的归因忠实度，并大幅减少了路径积分所需的评估次数。

Conclusion: 模型架构与归因方法的协同设计可以实现精确且高效的GNN可解释性，多项式形式的约束不仅不损害预测性能，反而使归因计算变得确定且高效。

Abstract: We study feature-level and node-level explanations for graph neural networks (GNNs) through the lens of Aumann-Shapley attribution. Path-integral methods such as Integrated Gradients provide an axiomatic formulation of attribution, but their practical use in deep GNNs typically relies on finite-sample numerical approximations to the path integral, requiring a trade-off between quadrature error and computational cost. This paper proposes APEX, a model-attribution co-design framework that makes the attribution integral exactly computable under a polynomial GNN architecture. The key component is PolyGIN, a GIN-style graph network whose message-passing, normalization, and transformation operations preserve a bounded multivariate polynomial form for scalar model scores, such as pre-softmax logits. We show that, for a PolyGIN with $L$ polynomial transformation blocks, the derivative along the attribution path has degree at most $2^L-1$. Therefore, Gauss--Legendre quadrature can evaluate the Aumann--Shapley path integral exactly, up to floating-point precision, with $2^{L-1}$ deterministic evaluation points. The resulting attributions can be computed at the feature level and then aggregated into node-level scores while preserving completeness. Experiments on synthetic and real-world graph benchmarks show that PolyGIN maintains competitive predictive performance, while the complete APEX framework achieves higher attribution fidelity than the compared baselines and substantially reduces the number of evaluations required for path integration.

</details>


### [185] [TOUR: A Trajectory-Level Unlearning Benchmark for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21111)
*Chaofan Pan,Lingfei Ren,Xiangyu Jiang,Yanhua Li,Xuemei Cao,Xiangkun Wang,Hao Yu,Wei Wei,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TOUR是一个用于评估离线强化学习中轨迹级遗忘的基准测试，通过多种攻击方法和参照对比，揭示了单一成员分数评估遗忘质量的不可靠性，结论依赖于非成员构建、重新训练校准、攻击家族、保留效用和诊断范围等因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习代理在固定行为轨迹上训练，训练后需要删除特定轨迹数据，但评估删除效果存在困难：低成员分数可能反映轨迹移除、残留记忆、或策略崩溃。现有方法缺乏统一评估标准。

Method: 提出TOUR基准，包含轨迹级划分、匹配非成员对照、重新训练参照、保留效用锚点、以及多攻击隐私审计。在D4RL运动任务和AntMaze扩展上进行实验，比较了重新训练、微调、GA+Refit、TrajDeleter等方法，并使用参考模型、阈值、偏差、等价性、动作误差、表示基和查询受限等多种攻击进行评估。

Result: 常见删除基线表现出环境依赖的隐私-效用行为；重新训练和微调提供比GA+Refit更强的保留效用参照；TrajDeleter是有用的比较器但在相同审计下并非一致更强；基于似然的单一成员分数可能夸大删除质量。

Conclusion: 在评估的设置中，关于离线强化学习遗忘的结论在单一分数审计下不稳定，依赖于匹配非成员构建、重新训练相对校准、攻击家族、保留效用以及诊断架构或组件级证据的显式范围。

Abstract: Offline Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents are trained on fixed behavioral trajectories, which makes trajectory-level deletion important when selected data must be removed after training. Evaluating such deletion is difficult because a lower membership score can reflect trajectory removal, residual memorization visible to another attack, or policy collapse that destroys useful behavior. We introduce Trajectory-level memOrization and Unlearning in offline RL (TOUR), a benchmark that combines trajectory-level partitioning, matched non-member controls, retraining references, retained-performance anchors, and multi-attack privacy auditing. Across D4RL locomotion experiments and an exploratory AntMaze extension, TOUR shows that common deletion baselines have environment-dependent privacy-utility behavior. Retraining and fine-tuning often provide stronger retained-utility references than uniform GA+Refit, while TrajDeleter remains a useful comparator but is not uniformly stronger under the same audit. Reference-model, threshold, deviation, equivalence, action-error, representation-based, and query-limited attacks further show that a single likelihood-based membership score can overstate deletion quality. In the evaluated settings, conclusions about offline RL unlearning are therefore not stable under single-score auditing. They depend on matched non-member construction, retraining-relative calibration, attack family, retained utility, and explicit scope for diagnostic architecture or component-level evidence.

</details>


### [186] [GlucoTune: A Unified Framework for Blood Glucose Preprocessing, Forecasting, and Benchmarking in Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21117)
*Davide Marelli,Giorgia Rigamonti,Mirko Paolo Barbato,Paolo Napoletano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoTune是一个用于1型糖尿病血糖时间序列数据预处理、建模和评估的可复现框架，通过可配置的YAML流水线、统一接口和标准化基准，解决了数据预处理缺乏标准化、复现困难和数据分享受限等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血糖时间序列数据预处理缺乏标准化流程和评估协议，导致研究难以复现和公平比较；同时由于隐私和许可限制，预处理后的医疗数据集难以重新分发，进一步加剧了这些问题。

Method: 提出了GlucoTune框架：1) 通过可移植的YAML配置文件定义可配置的预处理流水线，实现可复现的数据处理而不分发敏感数据；2) 提供统一的接口用于实现、训练和评估血糖预测模型；3) 集成公共数据集的标准封装器；4) 提供最新的血糖预测和通用时间序列预测方法的精选集合；5) 包含基准测试排行榜，支持跨数据集、预处理配置和预测方法的系统比较。

Result: 通过综合实验验证了框架的有效性，并通过用户研究评估了其可用性。

Conclusion: GlucoTune通过标准化从预处理到模型评估的完整实验工作流，实现了直接从原始数据集进行可复现实验，为糖尿病管理中的数据驱动方法研究提供了可靠、透明且可扩展的基准平台。

Abstract: Preprocessing blood glucose time-series data is a critical yet often overlooked step in developing data-driven methods for diabetes management, particularly for type 1 diabetes. The lack of standardized preprocessing workflows and evaluation protocols hinders reproducibility and complicates fair comparison across studies. These challenges are further exacerbated by data-sharing restrictions, as privacy and licensing constraints often prevent the redistribution of preprocessed medical datasets. To address these limitations, we present GlucoTune, a comprehensive and extensible framework for reproducible experimentation with blood glucose time-series data. The framework standardizes the entire experimental workflow, from preprocessing to model evaluation, enabling reproducible experiments directly from the original datasets. Reproducible preprocessing is achieved through configurable pipelines defined in portable YAML configuration files, ensuring consistent data handling without distributing sensitive preprocessed data. Beyond preprocessing, GlucoTune provides a unified interface for implementing, training, and evaluating blood glucose prediction models. The framework integrates public datasets through standardized wrappers and provides a curated collection of state-of-the-art blood glucose prediction and general time-series forecasting methods, while remaining readily extensible to additional datasets, preprocessing strategies, and forecasting models. To promote transparent and consistent evaluation, GlucoTune includes a benchmarking leaderboard that reports results across datasets, preprocessing configurations, and forecasting methods, enabling systematic comparison of experimental settings. We demonstrate the effectiveness of GlucoTune through comprehensive experiments and assess its usability in a user study.

</details>


### [187] [Relative Value Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21120)
*Marc Höftmann,Jan Robine,Stefan Harmeling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出相对价值学习（RV）框架，直接学习状态间的价值差分，而非绝对价值；设计成对贝尔曼算子，证明其为γ-压缩映射，推导出1步、n步和λ-return目标，并重构广义优势估计得到无偏策略梯度估计器R-GAE；与PPO集成后在Atari基准上表现具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中评论家通常估计绝对状态价值V(s)，但实际上只有价值差对控制决策相关。现有方法间接通过绝对价值之差获取相对信息，作者质疑是否有必要学习绝对价值，提出直接学习价值差分的框架。

Method: (1) 通过反对称函数Δ(s_i,s_j)=V(s_i)-V(s_j)直接建模价值差分；(2) 设计成对贝尔曼算子并证明其为γ-压缩映射且不动点唯一等于真实价值差分；(3) 推导1步、n步和λ-return目标；(4) 从成对差分重构GAE得到无偏策略梯度估计器R-GAE；(5) 将RV与PPO集成。

Result: 在Atari基准（49个ALE游戏）上，RV-PPO与标准PPO相比取得具有竞争力的表现，表明相对价值估计可作为绝对评论家的有效替代方案。

Conclusion: 直接学习相对价值差分而非绝对价值是可行的，且在控制任务中能取得与标准方法相当的竞争力表现。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning, critics typically estimate absolute state values $V(s)$, estimating how good a particular situation is in isolation. However, it turns out that only differences in value are relevant for control. Motivated by this, we propose Relative Value Learning (RV), a framework that learns value differences directly via an antisymmetric function $Δ(s_i, s_j) = V(s_i) - V(s_j)$. We introduce a pairwise Bellman operator and prove it is a $γ$-contraction with a unique fixed point equal to the true value differences, derive well-posed $1$-step, $n$-step and $λ$-return targets and reconstruct generalized advantage estimation from pairwise differences to obtain an unbiased policy-gradient estimator (R-GAE). Beyond theoretical results, we integrate RV with PPO and achieve competitive performance on the Atari benchmark (49 ALE games) compared to standard PPO, indicating that relative value estimation is an effective alternative to absolute critics.

</details>


### [188] [Demographically-Informed Heat-Mortality Risk Curves via Risk Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21131)
*Alex O. Davies,Eunice Lo,Rui Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出风险图神经网络(RGNN)，通过层次化GNN编码器利用人口普查特征优化DLNM系数向量，在保持可解释风险曲线输出的同时显著提升预测校准能力，在英格兰和威尔士10个区域的2022年热浪评估中表现优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLNM模型虽然能有效拟合温度-死亡率时间序列，但忽略了人口统计和地理背景信息，而这些因素已被证实与热脆弱性密切相关。作者希望弥合这一差距，在保持DLNM可解释性的同时引入上下文特征以提升预测性能。

Method: 提出Risk Graph Neural Networks (RGNNs)——一种层次化图神经网络编码器，利用细粒度人口普查特征来优化DLNM系数向量，从而在保持可解释风险曲线输出的同时改善预测校准。

Result: 在英格兰和威尔士10个区域的两个 unprecedented 高温年份评估中，RGNN变体在2022年热浪期间保持了较低的点误差和接近标称的不确定性覆盖，而基线模型则完全失效。

Conclusion: RGNN能有效整合人口地理上下文信息来增强热相关死亡风险预测，在保持模型可解释性的同时显著提升预测校准能力，特别是在极端热浪事件中表现稳健。

Abstract: Estimating heat-related mortality risk is a core task in environmental epidemiology, typically addressed with Distributed Lag Non-linear Models (DLNMs); interpretable exposure-response surfaces fitted to temperature-mortality time series. DLNMs are effective but ignore demographic and geographic context, despite well-established relevance to heat vulnerability. We propose Risk Graph Neural Networks (RGNNs), a hierarchical GNN encoder that uses granular census features to optimise DLNM coefficient vectors, preserving interpretable risk curve outputs while substantially improving predictive calibration. Evaluated across 10 regions of England and Wales on two unprecedented heat years, RGNN variants maintain both lower point-errors and near-nominal uncertainty coverage during the 2022 heatwave where baselines collapse.

</details>


### [189] [Agree on the Model, Verify the Inference: GKR Protocols for HND-Based Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21162)
*Xiaolong Liang,Juanjuan Li,Rui Qin,Yisheng Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GKR-HND协议，通过注册模型、GKR证明和同态-非同态分解，在假设诚实验证者和不共谋条件下实现Transformer推理的可验证委托，避免密集矩阵重放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外包Transformer推理面临模型替换和不完整执行的安全风险，而直接重放验证又丧失了委托计算的优势，需在安全性和效率间取得平衡。

Method: 采用注册模型协议（registered-model），结合GKR证明系统验证Transformer的多项式骨干网络；利用同态-非同态分解（HND）拆分计算；验证者仅检查GKR转录本和注册权重，将昂贵的公开评估委托给计算工人；通过签名绑定的请求-响应机制确保非共谋条件下的可靠性。

Result: 在预训练HND模型上的实验验证了证明路径的有效性，并实现了委托公开计算，无需密集矩阵重放。

Conclusion: GKR-HND在可信验证者和非共谋假设下，能够在不牺牲委托计算效率的前提下，有效检测模型替换和不完整执行等攻击。

Abstract: Outsourced Transformer inference exposes clients to model substitution and incomplete execution, while direct replay removes the computational benefit of delegation. We present GKR-HND, a registered-model protocol for verifying the polynomial backbone of Homomorphic--Nonhomomorphic Decomposition Transformers. The retained verifier checks the GKR transcript and registered-weight openings, but delegates expensive public evaluations to an assigned computation worker. Assuming an honest retained verifier and prover--worker non-collusion, the verifier accepts only when the worker's signed, request-bound response agrees with the proof claims. Experiments with pretrained HND models validate the proof path and the delegated public computation without dense-matrix replay.

</details>


### [190] [Automated Synthesis and Adversarial Validation of Executable Causal Research Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21173)
*Irena Girshovitz,Dan Zeltzer,Ran Gilad-Bachrach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARA是一个基于AI的流行病学研究助手框架，通过显式编码因果设计原则、构建结构化协议和生成合成数据，使自动化研究中的隐性因果假设失败变得可见。尽管在数值准确性上未显著优于标准LLM方法，但ARA能有效改变失败模式——从默默返回因果估计转变为主动暴露协议问题、诊断失败或降级为非因果解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究系统虽然加速了实证分析，但容易出现"静默失败"——代码执行成功但依赖无效的因果假设。现有系统缺乏对这种隐性失败的可视化机制，因此需要一种能够显式暴露因果假设问题的框架。

Method: ARA将自然语言研究问题转化为结构化因果协议和可执行分析代码，流程包括：(1) 协议构建，(2) 使用结构因果模型(SCMs)生成合成数据集（支持在受限数据环境下开发），(3) 在识别假设受控违反条件下进行对抗性验证，评估分析结果。

Result: 在自动因果推理基准上评估发现：协议构建和对抗性验证并未持续提升与基准估计的数值一致性。但关键变化在于失败模式的转变——ARA不是默默返回因果估计，而是主动暴露协议问题、诊断失败、不完整推理或将结论降级为非因果解释。

Conclusion: 以有效性为先的自动化科学系统不应仅以答案准确性评估，还应评估系统在因果主张不成立时是否能够主动发出警示。

Abstract: While automated research systems promise to accelerate empirical analysis, they are prone to silent failures: instances in which analysis code executes successfully yet relies on invalid causal assumptions. We present the Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based Epidemiology Research Assistant (ARA), a framework that makes these failures visible by explicitly encoding causal design principles, study-specific assumptions, and methodological constraints. ARA integrates protocol construction, synthetic data generation, and adversarial validation into a unified pipeline. The framework translates natural language research questions into structured causal protocols and executable analysis code by first constructing a protocol and then generating synthetic datasets using Structural Causal Models (SCMs) with known ground-truth effects. This synthetic-data step can also support pipeline development when access to confidential data, such as medical data, is restricted. The generated analysis is then evaluated under controlled violations of identification assumptions. We evaluate ARA on the Automated Causal Reasoning Benchmark, assessing recovery of identification strategies, causal quantities, treatment and outcome variables, and consistency between generated code and approved protocol. Protocol construction and adversarial validation did not consistently improve numerical agreement with benchmark estimates compared with standard LLM-based generation. However, they changed the failure mode: instead of silently returning causal estimates, ARA often surfaced protocol concerns, diagnostic failures, incomplete inference, or downgraded non-causal interpretations. These findings suggest that validity-first automated science systems should be evaluated not only by answer accuracy, but also by whether they indicate when causal claims are unwarranted.

</details>


### [191] [Filter Learning for Subgraphs: Algebras and Performance Risk Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21263)
*Purui Zhang,Feng Ji,Yanan Zhao,Bihan Wen,Wee Peng Tay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出子图滤波器学习（SFL）框架，利用距离感知拉普拉斯构建子图滤波器代数，在仅部分观测图拓扑的情况下逼近全局图滤波器，并建立了风险界理论保证，实验证明优于多项式滤波器和直接数值学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中图拓扑往往不完全可知，而传统谱图信号处理任务通常假设完整图结构可获取，需要在不完整观测下实现有效的图滤波。

Method: 将SFL形式化为统计学习问题，基于距离感知拉普拉斯构造构建子图滤波器代数，定义结构化的可控滤波器类用于逼近，并在最小二乘损失下建立性能风险界。

Result: 在真实数据集上，所提代数模型在SFL任务中一致优于多项式滤波器、分布无关算子以及直接数值滤波器学习基线。

Conclusion: 子图滤波器代数能够有效学习部分观测下的最优滤波算子，并具有良好的理论保证和实际性能。

Abstract: Graph signal processing tasks that leverage spectral information typically assume access to the complete graph topology, which is often unavailable in practice. We propose a systematic framework for subgraph filter learning (SFL), where subgraph-supported operators approximate ambient graph filters under partial observations. We formulate SFL as a statistical learning problem in which optimal subgraph operators are inherently data-dependent. To address the difficulty of directly estimating such operators, we develop a subgraph filter algebra based on distance-aware Laplacian constructions, defining a structured and controllable class of filters for effective approximation. We further establish performance risk bounds under the least squares loss, quantifying how well the learned operator approximates the restricted ambient mapping. Experiments real-world datasets show that, for SFL tasks, the proposed algebraic models consistently outperform polynomial filters, distribution-agnostic operators, and direct numerical filter learning baselines that attempt to recover the underlying structure from data.

</details>


### [192] [The Dark Room in the Reward Channel: Dense Prediction Rewards Collapse GRPO-Trained LLM Agents -- and What Actually Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21273)
*Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了在组归一化强化学习（GRPO）框架下，基于预测的密集奖励（预测下一观测）会灾难性地摧毁LLM智能体的策略，导致其陷入"暗室"病态（预测准确率趋近1.0但任务成功率为0）。问题根源在于GRPO的z-score标准化放大了密集信号的组内方差，使有界奖励变为无界压力。移除标准差归一化即可恢复性能，而使用辅助损失通道则能带来约20个百分点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集的每步监督似乎是解决稀疏奖励、长程LLM智能体问题的有吸引力的方法：通过奖励智能体预测其下一观测来引导记忆形成。但作者发现这一方案在GRPO下不仅失败，还会摧毁策略，因此需要探究其原因并提供解决方案。

Method: 在ALFWorld环境上使用Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B模型，使用基于势能的预测奖励进行GRPO训练。通过单因素消融实验定位原因（移除标准差归一化），并提出双行解释：全失败组中z-score优势对塑造系数不变，有界奖励变为无界压力。进一步通过控制信号传递矩阵实验（相同信号，不同消费机制）对比奖励通道和辅助损失通道的效果。

Result: 每个实验都陷入退化吸收态（预测准确率->1.0，任务成功率->0，回合长度固定为horizon）。移除GRPO的标准差归一化后，相同的预测奖励从灾难性（0%）变为与基线相当。辅助损失通道带来约20个百分点的提升，且混淆的金标签（shuffled-gold placebo）与真实金标签效果相当。方差分布准则能反推崩溃现象并携带预注册预测。

Conclusion: 在GRPO下使用密集预测奖励是灾难性的，根源在于z-score标准化放大了密集信号的组内方差。解决方案是使用辅助损失通道而非奖励通道来传递相同信号。方差分布准则可判断哪些信号是"结构安全的"。该发现具有重要实践意义：奖励通道充其量是中性的，而辅助损失通道能带来显著提升。

Abstract: Dense per-step supervision is an appealing remedy for sparse-reward, long-horizon LLM agents: reward the agent for predicting its next observation, and memory should follow. We show that under group-normalized RL (GRPO), this recipe does not merely fail -- it destroys the policy. Across Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B on ALFWorld, a potential-based prediction reward drives every run into a degenerate absorbing state (prediction accuracy -> 1.0, task success -> 0,episode length pinned at the horizon): the "dark room" pathology, built automatically by the optimizer. A single-factor ablation localizes the cause -- removing only GRPO's std normalization turns the same reward from catastrophic (0%) into baseline parity -- and a two-line proposition explains why: in all-fail groups the z-scored advantage is invariant to the shaping coefficient, so bounded rewards become unbounded pressure and annealing cannot help. Our central insight generalizes this: what z-scoring amplifies is a dense signal's within-group variance while all-fail groups dominate, so signals whose variance decays by mastery are structurally amplifier-safe.This variance-profile criterion retrodicts our collapses, carries preregistered predictions for arms that had not yet run, and is consistent with published reward-channel successes (a compatibility check, not an independent test). Finally, a controlled signal-delivery matrix (identical signal, varying only the consumption mechanism) shows the reward channel is at best neutral while the auxiliary-loss channel gains ~20 points -- and a shuffled-gold placebo matches the true-gold arm, so the gap survives without correct labels. Endpoints are single-seed; seed replication and group-size controls are preregistered and in progress.

</details>


### [193] [Multi-Task Learning for Heterogeneous Prediction from Video Game State with Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21290)
*Jonas Peché,Aliaksei Tsishurou,Alexander Zap,Günter Wallner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在团队多人游戏中，多任务学习是否比单任务模型能更好地提升泛化能力，同时降低训练和推理成本。实验基于《坦克世界》大规模数据集，采用融合栅格化视觉、全局上下文和逐单元状态的多模态架构，比较了单任务与多任务训练、损失加权策略、梯度冲突处理、预训练/微调以及跨地图迁移的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代游戏遥测数据从同一结构化观测中提供多个相关的监督信号，自然适合多任务学习。然而，专门化的单任务模型可能无法充分利用这些共享信息。作者希望验证多任务学习在团队多人游戏预测任务中能否比单任务模型取得更好的泛化性能，同时降低计算成本。

Method: 采用多模态架构，将栅格化视觉输入、全局匹配上下文和逐单元状态信息通过图像编码器与注意力机制进行融合建模。针对多任务学习，实验比较了单任务与多任务训练、混合损失加权策略、冲突梯度处理方法、预训练/微调（在有限目标数据场景下）以及跨游戏地图的结构化环境迁移。

Result: 在《坦克世界》大规模数据集上进行了系统实验，比较了单任务和多任务训练的效果，评估了不同加权策略和梯度冲突处理方法，验证了预训练/微调在有限目标数据下的表现，并分析了跨地图迁移的性能。

Conclusion: 多任务学习在团队多人游戏预测任务中能够提升泛化能力并降低训练和推理成本；适当的损失加权和梯度冲突处理对多任务性能至关重要；预训练/微调在数据有限场景下有效；模型在结构化环境变化（跨地图）下具有一定的迁移能力。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) is a promising approach for prediction tasks derived from video game state data, as modern game telemetry provides multiple related supervision signals from the same structured observations. We study whether a shared model trained jointly across tasks in team-based multiplayer games can improve generalization while reducing training and inference cost compared to specialized single-task models. We adapt a multimodal architecture for endpoint prediction to a general multi-task setting that combines rasterized vision inputs, global match context, and per-unit state information through an image encoder and attention-based interaction modeling. Experiments on a large proprietary World of Tanks dataset compare single-task and multi-task training, evaluate weighting strategies for mixed losses and conflicting gradients, and test pre-training/fine-tuning under limited target-data regimes. We also examine within-game transfer across game maps under structured environment shift.

</details>


### [194] [AI Assistants Overassist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21306)
*Verona Teo,Raghav Jain,Tobias Gerstenberg,Max Kleiman-Weiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Int-Bench，一个基于模拟的基准测试，用于评估LLM在学习场景中的干预决策。研究发现LLM教师比人类更频繁、更早地进行干预，且倾向于提供完整解决方案而非针对性提示，这表明当前LLM助教更优化短期成功而非支持深层学习所需的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地被用作导师和思维伙伴，但它们在问题解决过程中如何做出干预决策（如干预时机、频率和方式）尚不明确。过度或过早的干预可能阻碍真正学习和认知参与，因此需要系统评估LLM的干预行为。

Method: 提出了Int-Bench仿真基准测试，模拟"学生"解决问题时"教师"监控推理过程并决定是否、何时以及如何干预。在代码调试、数学和脑筋急转弯三个领域评估LLM教师的干预频率、时机及其对即时任务成功和泛化的影响，并与人类进行对比。

Result: LLM比人类干预更频繁、更早；与人类不同，LLM倾向于提供完整解决方案而非针对性提示。

Conclusion: 当前LLM助教往往优化短期任务成功，而非支持深层学习所需的推理过程和长期成功。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as tutors and thought partners, helping users reason through problems. While guidance from AI assistants can scaffold thinking and foster learning, such benefits depend on how they help--for instance, intervening too early or too frequently may hinder true learning and cognitive engagement. Yet how AI systems navigate intervention decisions during problem-solving remains poorly understood. Here, we introduce Int-Bench, a simulation-based benchmark for evaluating LLM interventions during learning. Int-Bench simulates a "student" solving a problem while a "teacher" monitors the student's reasoning and decides whether, when, and how to intervene. Across three domains--code debugging, mathematics, and brain teasers--we evaluate LLM teachers on the frequency and timing of interventions, as well as their impact on both immediate task success and generalization to new problems. We also compare LLMs to humans, finding that LLMs intervene more frequently and earlier than humans. Moreover, in contrast to humans, they tend to provide complete solutions rather than targeted hints. These findings suggest that current LLM assistants often optimize for short-term success rather than supporting the reasoning processes needed for deeper learning and long-term success.

</details>


### [195] [M$^3$-Gen: Interpretable Multimodal Generation of Gene Expression Profiles Using Clinical and Imaging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21343)
*Francesca Pia Panaccione,Carlo Sgaravatti,Marco Venere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M$^3$-Gen是一个多模态生成框架，利用条件GAN结合组织病理学图像和临床元数据生成基因表达谱，并通过注意力机制提供内在可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据获取受限于高成本和隐私问题，阻碍了多模态研究和AI驱动应用的发展，因此需要一种能够从易获取的临床元数据和组织病理学图像中生成基因表达谱的方法。

Method: 采用条件生成对抗网络（GAN），通过对比学习从临床变量和组织病理学图像中学习统一潜在表示，并利用双模态嵌入指导基因表达谱的生成；同时引入基于注意力的多模态融合机制以提供可解释性。

Result: 在TCGA数据集上的评估表明，M$^3$-Gen能够生成逼真且功能上有意义的基因表达数据，并能识别组织病理学图像中对特定基因表达谱生成影响最强的区域。

Conclusion: M$^3$-Gen通过多模态条件生成有效解决了基因表达数据不足的问题，同时其内在可解释性机制使模型决策可被理解，为多模态生物医学研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous biomedical data, including clinical metadata, histopathology images, and molecular profiles, is crucial for comprehensive disease understanding. However, gene expression data acquisition remains constrained by high costs and privacy concerns, limiting its use in multimodal research and AI-driven applications. We present MultiModal Molecular Generation (M$^3$-Gen), a novel framework for the generation of gene expression profiles by conditioning a Generative Adversarial Network on histopathology images and clinical metadata. M$^3$-Gen learns a unified latent representation from the clinical variables and the images, leveraging contrastive learning, and exploits the embeddings of the two modalities to guide a generative model in producing biologically coherent gene expression profiles. Evaluations on the TCGA dataset demonstrate that M$^3$-Gen generates realistic and functionally meaningful gene expression data. Importantly, by integrating multiple modalities in an attention-based mechanism, M$^3$-Gen provides intrinsic explainability: it allows the identification of which regions of the histopathology images most strongly influenced the generation of specific gene expression profiles, making the model's decisions interpretable by design.

</details>


### [196] [How Many Bits Can an Adapter Write? Measuring the Capacity and Memorization of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21351)
*Kaizhen Tan,Heqing Du,Yang Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过压缩理论方法，直接测量LoRA适配器在冻结基座模型中写入的信息量（以比特为单位），发现其信息存储能力远小于全参数微调，且主要取决于参数位置而非数量。研究表明，适配器写入的比特数比参数数量更能预测隐私泄漏风险，并首次在监督学习与强化学习微调之间划出清晰界限——基于可验证奖励训练的适配器不会记录训练数据中的秘密信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战学术界普遍将LoRA适配器视为"技能"而非数据记录的隐含假设，通过量化分析揭示适配器实际存储的信息量及其与隐私泄漏的关系。

Method: 将压缩理论中的记忆化分析方法扩展至冻结基座模型场景，直接测量低秩适配器在不变模型中写入的比特数。通过控制参数位置（注意力层vs MLP层）和基座结构完整性进行对比实验。

Result: 1) 适配器每个可训练参数仅存储几比特信息，远低于全参数微调；2) 参数位置比参数数量更关键：相同参数量从注意力层移至MLP层，信息容量几乎翻倍；3) 移除基座模型结构，适配器容量几乎消失；4) 隐私泄漏与适配器写入的比特数正相关；5) 监督微调会逐字复制秘密信息，而基于可验证奖励训练的强化学习适配器不会记录训练数据中的秘密。

Conclusion: 将关于微调信息存储的直觉性认知转化为可设计、可量化的指标，为隐私保护和模型安全设计提供了理论依据和实用工具。

Abstract: A LoRA adapter is a few megabytes that almost everyone treats as a skill rather than a record of the data behind it. We put that assumption on a scale. Extending compression-based memorization analysis to the frozen-base setting, we measure directly, in bits, how much a low-rank adapter writes into a model it never changes. The answer is both smaller than full fine-tuning and less lawful than parameter counting would predict. Adapters store a couple of bits per trainable parameter, well short of a full model's budget, but that figure turns less on how many parameters an adapter carries than on where they sit. Move the same parameter budget from attention into the MLP and it holds nearly twice as much; strip the frozen base of its structure and the capacity all but disappears. Applied to realistic fine-tunes of Qwen2.5, the same instrument shows privacy leakage rising with the bits an adapter writes rather than the parameters it nominally has, and it draws a clean line between supervised and reinforcement learning: the secrets that supervised fine-tuning copies down verbatim, an adapter trained on verifiable rewards never records. Measuring what fine-tuning writes, rather than attacking it after the fact, turns a piece of folklore into a quantity one can design against.

</details>


### [197] [Gradient Concentration, Not Weight Saliency, Explains Representation-Level Class Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21353)
*Billel Habbati,Alessio Merlo,Luca Verderame,Meriem Guerar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对SalUn中的显著性掩码机制进行了首次受控消融实验，发现在CIFAR-10/100和ResNet-18设置下，基于显著性的权重选择与随机掩码及无约束更新在表征级遗忘效果上统计等价。遗忘梯度高度集中在最后几层（约92%），显著性掩码的类别特异性极低（0.09-0.11）。结果表明表征级遗忘主要由梯度集中度和表征几何决定，而非显著性选择的权重身份，呼吁直接作用于潜在表征的遗忘目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于显著性选择参数子集进行遗忘更新的方法被广泛采用，但显著性掩码对表征级遗忘的实际贡献尚不清楚。本文旨在通过受控消融实验揭示其真实作用。

Method: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上使用ResNet-18，采用匹配计算量的实验设计，比较显著性掩码、同等稀疏度的随机掩码和无约束更新三种策略，保持遗忘目标、优化计划和计算预算固定。使用线性探针、原型恢复和逐层CKA等多种表征级评估方法。

Result: 三种配置在表征级可恢复性上统计等价；遗忘梯度在应用任何掩码前约92%集中在最后几层；显著性掩码的类别特异性指数仅为0.09-0.11，不同遗忘类别选择的参数子集高度重叠。

Conclusion: 在所研究设置中，表征级遗忘主要由梯度集中度和表征几何驱动，而非显著性选择的权重身份。有效表征级遗忘需要直接作用于潜在表征的目标，而非更复杂的权重选择策略。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data while preserving model utility. Many state-of-the-art approaches pursue this goal by restricting the forgetting update to a subset of parameters selected through gradient-based saliency. Although such methods are widely adopted, the actual contribution of saliency-based weight selection to representation-level forgetting remains unclear. In this work, we perform the first controlled ablation of the saliency masking mechanism used by SalUn. Using a matched-compute experimental design on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with ResNet-18, we compare saliency-based masking against random masks of equal sparsity and unconstrained updates, while keeping the unlearning objective, optimization schedule, and computational budget fixed. Across multiple representation-level evaluations, including linear probing, prototype recovery, and layer-wise CKA, the three configurations exhibit statistically equivalent representation-level recoverability. We find that forget gradients are strongly concentrated in the final network layers (approximately 92% of the squared gradient energy on CIFAR-10) before any mask is applied, causing all masking strategies to operate within the same representational subspace. Furthermore, saliency masks show limited class specificity (specificity index 0.09-0.11), selecting highly overlapping parameter subsets across different forget classes. Our findings suggest that, in the studied setting, representation-level forgetting is primarily governed by gradient concentration and representation geometry rather than by the specific identity of saliency-selected weights. More broadly, the results support a growing body of evidence indicating that effective representation-level unlearning requires objectives that act directly on latent representations rather than on increasingly sophisticated weight-selection strategies.

</details>


### [198] [Emergent Misalignment Recruits a Pre-existing Persona Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21356)
*Mohammed Suhail B Nadaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct模型中发现，窄领域微调（如不安全代码）会通过激活微调前已存在的低秩人格子空间（persona subspace）导致广泛对齐失效。该子空间跨4个无关领域共享同一核心，从残差流中投影移除可完全消除广泛失调（27.7%→0.0%），注入则诱导失调达45.4%。后验权重编辑无法根除该结构，且恶意数据跨领域分布时失调效应远超机械权重叠加与多样性匹配的预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究窄领域微调（如不安全代码）为何会导致模型在与训练数据无关的问题上出现广泛对齐失效（emergent misalignment），并揭示其背后的机制。

Method: 采用对比教师强制（contrastive teacher forcing）从冻结的指令微调模型Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct中提取每领域人格子空间；通过子空间投影/注入实验、优化步骤梯度分析、后验权重编辑、多领域数据分布实验等手段进行因果验证。

Result: 发现4个无关领域共享一个低秩核心（信噪比657倍于随机子空间）；第一步优化即沿广泛失调方向更新；残差流投影移除可将失调从27.7%降至0.0%，注入则诱导失调达45.4%；后验编辑无法根除；跨领域分布数据产生超预期失调。

Conclusion: 窄领域微调导致的广泛对齐失效源于模型内预先存在的人格子空间结构，该结构低秩、跨领域共享、且难以通过权重编辑移除；残差流投影可有效预防但会同时消除目标行为。

Abstract: Fine-tuning an aligned language model on a narrow stream of bad advice can make it broadly misaligned on questions unrelated to the training data, a phenomenon called emergent misalignment. We ask why the narrow lesson generalizes at all, and we find that narrow fine-tuning recruits a persona structure that is present in the model before the fine-tune exists. From a frozen instruction-tuned model (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct) we extract per-domain persona subspaces by contrastive teacher forcing and find that 4 unrelated domains share one low-rank core at 657x a random-subspace null, with 82% of that core lying outside a style core built at matched diversity. The literal first optimizer step of fine-tuning on insecure code climbs a broad-misalignment margin harder than the same code framed as educational, and forecasts realized margin movement out to 375 steps. Projecting the subspace out of the residual stream throughout fine-tuning prevents broad misalignment (27.7% to 0.0% of judged generations) while a matched-rank random subspace changes nothing; injecting it into the never-fine-tuned model induces misalignment that grows with dose to 45.4%, past the fine-tuned model it is measured against. The same projection applied to the weight gradient is inert, and three post-hoc weight edits leave the disposition in place: the sharpest edit suppresses the behavior rather than removing it, and the ablated structure re-forms inside the subspace the edit cleared. Spreading a fixed budget of bad data across 4 domains produces more broad misalignment than mechanical weight superposition and matched diversity jointly account for. All measurements come from one model at 14B; the extraction is from an aligned instruction-tuned checkpoint, which leaves the structure's provenance open; and the intervention that prevents misalignment also abolishes the narrow trained behavior.

</details>


### [199] [Mean-to-Score Discrete Diffusion: Posterior-Mean Denoisers for Score Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21372)
*Jingyuan Li,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yixuan Jiang,Wei Liu,Yi Zhu,Zuoqiang Shi,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出离散扩散模型中的得分比率虽保证非负性，但不保证贝叶斯可实现性，导致采样中出现负权重。作者提出“均值到得分”（M2S）方法，通过预测干净令牌的后验均值并转换为得分，在多个任务上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型（如SEDD）虽通过正得分比率保证非负跳转率，但未约束得分比率必须由某个有效后验分布诱导，导致实际训练出的模型得分向量违反坐标盒约束或与有效后验不可兼容，进而产生负预归一化权重，损害生成质量。

Method: 提出Mean-to-Score（M2S）框架：预测干净令牌的后验均值，然后通过精确的、依赖于核函数的线性映射将其转换为得分。该构造适用于任何满足温和支撑条件的逐坐标CTMC。对于均匀噪声，该映射将概率单纯形映射到桥多面体上；对于吸收掩码噪声，M2S恢复MD4目标。

Result: 1）将原始得分投影到桥多面体上，消除了所有观测到的负权重，生成PPL从203.6降至175.1；2）在CIFAR-10上（28.4M参数），M2S将测试BPD从3.173降至3.129，FID-50k显著改善；3）在OpenWebText（170M参数）上，M2S以128步达到生成PPL 143.3，优于最强均匀基线SEDD的183.6。

Conclusion: M2S通过强制得分满足贝叶斯可实现性约束，有效解决了离散扩散模型中的得分不一致问题，在不改变采样器的情况下显著提升了生成质量，并在多个基准上超越了现有方法。

Abstract: Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD) parameterizes discrete reverse processes with unconstrained positive score ratios. While positivity guarantees nonnegative reverse jump rates, it does not ensure Bayes realizability: ratios at a noisy state need not be jointly induced by any clean-token posterior under the forward kernel. The score-entropy loss has the correct population optimum but does not enforce this constraint away from it. In a trained pure-uniform SEDD checkpoint, roughly one quarter of complete score vectors violate the coordinate box, while more than half lie inside it yet remain materially incompatible with any valid posterior. Such violations can produce negative pre-normalization weights in finite-step sampling. Projecting raw scores onto the bridge polytope removes all observed negative weights and improves external generative PPL from $203.6$ to $175.1$ without changing the sampler. We introduce \emph{mean-to-score} (M2S), which predicts a clean-token posterior mean and converts it to the score through an exact kernel-dependent linear map. The construction applies to any known coordinate-wise continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) satisfying a mild support condition. For uniform corruption, it maps the probability simplex onto the bridge polytope; for absorbing-mask corruption, the resulting objective recovers MD4 exactly. In a controlled 28.4M-parameter CIFAR-10 comparison, M2S lowers test BPD from $3.173$ to $3.129$ and FID-50k from $\CifarSEDDFID$ to $\CifarMtwoSFID$. A 170M-parameter M2S model trained on about 262B OpenWebText token slots outperforms the evaluated pure-uniform SEDD, GIDD, and Neural CTMC checkpoints at every tested sampling budget, reaching generative PPL $143.3$ at 128 steps versus $183.6$ for the strongest pure-uniform baseline.

</details>


### [200] [A Diffusion-Model Subpopulation Digital Twin for Mobile Health Deployment: A Case Study on the HeartSteps Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21403)
*Ziping Xu,Yuyi Chang,Chenshun Ni,Nithin Sugavanam,Asim H. Gazi,Pedja Klasnja,Emre Ertin,Susan A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"JITAI-Twins"方法，利用条件时间序列扩散模型，通过三步构建（预训练、微调、推理时校准）目标子群体的数字孪生体，用于在JITAI部署前验证在线学习算法。在HeartSteps系列研究中验证表明，该方法能比简单模拟器更好地再现目标子群体的时间序列和参与者间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动健康干预中在线学习算法设计不当可能给参与者带来负担并导致脱退，因此在实际部署前需要利用逼真的模拟用户来评估候选算法。

Method: 基于条件时间序列扩散模型，确保时间一致性（未来行为不影响已生成的过去）。分三步构建：1）在大规模观测数据上预训练；2）在相关人群的先前干预部署数据上微调；3）利用领域科学家专业知识对下一目标群体进行推理时校准。

Result: 在HeartSteps系列（v2至v4）的物理活动建议干预部署中验证，该方法在再现目标子群体的时间结构和参与者间结构方面优于简单模拟器。

Conclusion: 所提出的数字孪生体可用于在目标部署运行前进行模拟，这是测试和为在线算法设计决策提供信息的前提条件。

Abstract: Mobile-health interventions increasingly use online learning and decision making algorithms to personalize when to nudge users toward healthier behavior, but a poorly designed algorithm can burden and disengage participants. New algorithm design decisions should therefore be vetted against realistic simulated users before each real-life deployment. We propose a method to develop ``JITAI-Twins'': digital twins of a target subpopulation for comparing candidate online algorithms before a just-in-time adaptive intervention (JITAI) deployment. The method builds on a conditional time-series diffusion model that is temporally consistent (future actions do not affect the generated past), and it supports repeated updating from three sources of information, in three steps: pre-training on a large observational dataset, fine-tuning on small prior intervention deployments in related populations, and inference-time calibration to the next target population from domain-scientist expertise. We validate the twin at each pre-deployment stage of the long-running HeartSteps series (v2 through v4) of physical-activity suggestion intervention deployments, treating each successive deployment as an upcoming study. The proposed method reproduces the target subpopulation's temporal and between-participant structure better than simpler simulators. These results suggest that our twin can be used to simulate a target deployment before it runs, the prerequisite for testing and informing online algorithm design decisions.

</details>


### [201] [Context-weighted Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21427)
*Daniil Cherniavskii,Daniel Severo,Karen Ullrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对离散流匹配模型中标准因子化训练目标导致模型面对不同难度令牌的问题，提出基于局部上下文信息的连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）改进方法。上下文加权采样器在几乎无计算开销下提升生成质量，缩放交叉熵损失函数在OpenWebText上降低高达63%的生成困惑度，并匹配半自回归块扩散基线质量，同时保持任意顺序生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散流匹配的标准因子化训练目标使模型同时面对条件良好、可预测的令牌和模糊、高熵的令牌，导致训练信号质量不均。作者观察到令牌值的不确定性与邻域可用上下文密度密切相关，因此希望通过引入局部上下文信息来改进训练和采样过程。

Method: 1) 基于局部上下文密度与令牌不确定性关系的经验观察，提出上下文加权采样器，在采样时对连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）进行简单修改以融入局部上下文信息；2) 提出缩放交叉熵损失函数，对不同令牌的训练信号进行重新加权，缓解训练目标中难易样本不均衡问题。

Result: 上下文加权采样器在几乎无计算开销下提升生成质量；缩放交叉熵损失在OpenWebText上降低生成困惑度高达63%；方法在质量上匹配强半自回归块扩散基线，同时保持任意顺序生成能力。

Conclusion: 局部上下文是离散生成建模中的一个重要因素，简单的上下文感知修改可以显著提升采样和训练效率。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching provides a flexible framework for generative modeling on discrete structures. However, the standard factorized training objective exposes the model to targets of varying difficulty, mixing well-conditioned, predictable tokens with ambiguous, high-entropy ones. We empirically demonstrate that the uncertainty over the value of each token is closely related to the density of available context in its neighborhood. Motivated by this observation, we propose a simple modification to the underlying continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) that incorporates local context information. Our context-weighted sampler improves generation quality with negligible computational overhead, while our scaled cross-entropy loss function reweights the training signal from different tokens and reduces generative perplexity by up to 63% on OpenWebText. Moreover, our approach matches a strong semi-autoregressive block diffusion baseline in quality while retaining the ability to perform generation in any order. These results highlight the role of local context as an important factor in discrete generative modeling and show that simple context-aware modifications can significantly improve both sampling and training efficiency.

</details>


### [202] [KroQuant: Kronecker-Structured Block Transforms for Efficient Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21446)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KroQuant提出了一种基于Kronecker结构的可逆变换方法，用于扩散Transformer的W4A4后训练量化。该方法对每32个元素的激活块应用可逆变换，参数量少于逐通道缩放，推理速度比SmoothQuant快14%，在PixArt-Σ、SANA和FLUX.1-schnell等模型上取得了优于SVDQuant和LoRaQ的量化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在W4A4量化下质量严重下降，原因是激活值存在异常值，4-bit格式无法有效表示。现有方法在量化质量与推理成本之间存在权衡，缺乏既能保证量化精度又具有低推理开销的方案。

Method: KroQuant方法包含两个核心组件：1）对每32个元素的激活块应用学习得到的Kronecker结构可逆变换，块局部结构可运行小型tensor-core GEMM；2）离线LoRaQ权重校准，吸收剩余逐权重量化误差。

Result: 在MI350 GPU上，KroQuant量化核比SmoothQuant核快14%；在PixArt-Σ、SANA、FLUX.1-schnell的W4A4（MXFP4e2）设置下，KroQuant在MJHQ-30K和SDCI数据集上产生的输出比SVDQuant和LoRaQ更接近FP参考，且保持或提升了图像质量。

Conclusion: KroQuant通过Kronecker结构可逆变换和离线权重校准，在W4A4量化中实现了优于现有方法的量化质量与推理效率的平衡。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of diffusion transformers (DiTs) to W4A4 severely degrades output quality, because activations entering each linear layer contain outliers that 4-bit formats cannot represent. The standard fix applies an invertible linear transform to the activations and its inverse to the weights before quantizing both. Normalization layers between blocks force this transform to run online at every denoising step, making its inference computation cost the binding design constraint. Existing options trade quantization quality for inference cost: per-channel scaling (SmoothQuant) is computationally cheap but impacts the magnitude of the channels, which can harm quantization accuracy; fixed Hadamard transforms yield better quantization accuracy but require large block sizes that incur a high online cost; learned full-$d$ invertible transforms calibrate best but entail a prohibitive dense $d \times d$ matrix multiplication (GEMM) per layer per step.
  We propose KroQuant, a PTQ method that applies a learned Kronecker-structured invertible transform to each 32-element block of the activation, storing less than half the parameters of per-channel scaling. The block-local structure runs as small tensor-core GEMMs, and on an MI350 GPU the KroQuant quantizer kernel is up to $14\%$ faster than the SmoothQuant kernel. Offline LoRaQ weight calibration then absorbs the residual per-weight quantization error. On PixArt-$Σ$, SANA, and FLUX.1-schnell at W4A4 (MXFP4e2), KroQuant produces outputs closer to the FP reference than SVDQuant and LoRaQ on MJHQ-30K and SDCI, while preserving or improving image quality.

</details>


### [203] [Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21453)
*Rajiv Shailesh Chitale,Rahul Madhavan,Taneesh Gupta,Deepanway Ghosal,Aravindan Raghuveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTEL（Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization），一种在推理时通过token级错误定位来提升大语言模型性能的算法。TTEL利用环境反馈对比条件概率，精准定位错误步骤，截断轨迹并分支重新生成，最大化复用有效前缀。实验表明，TTEL在多个推理基准上（LiveCodeBench、AIME-2025、HMMT-2025）建立了严格支配的帕累托前沿，相比独立采样等基线方法，在更少的token消耗下取得了更高的性能（如Qwen3-8B在LiveCodeBench上pass@64达71.0%，token消耗仅为独立采样的约一半）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时扩展方法（如独立采样、多轮精炼）缺乏token级别的信用分配，导致有效的推理前缀经常被丢弃，造成计算效率低下。作者希望提出一种能在token级别精确定位错误并最大化复用有效推理前缀的算法，以提高推理时的计算效率。

Method: TTEL算法利用固定反馈或环境反馈进行token级错误定位。具体地，通过对比有信息反馈下的条件概率与空上下文基线的条件概率，隔离出错误发生的步骤。然后截断该步骤之后的轨迹，从该点分支生成新的内容，从而最大化复用正确的推理前缀。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench上，Qwen3-8B使用TTEL达到pass@64为71.0%，生成约360.4k tokens，而独立采样为735.0k tokens。在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025数学基准上，TTEL在使用Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507模型时均优于其他推理时基线方法。TTEL在多个序列推理领域建立了严格支配的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: TTEL通过token级错误定位实现了更高效的推理时计算扩展，在代码和数学推理任务上以更少的token消耗取得了更优的性能，验证了精细化信用分配在推理时扩展中的有效性。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time computation has emerged as a reliable method to improve the performance of large language models on complex reasoning and programming tasks. However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [204] [Error Certificates for KV-Cache Eviction via Randomized Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21475)
*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明确定性KV缓存驱逐无法感知被丢弃信息对注意力输出的影响，而随机化驱逐（泊松采样尾部+哈耶克校正）可实现可识别的误差认证。实验表明随机化带来的认证在预测失败上效果有限，但能有效区分缓存导致与模型固有的失败（AUC 0.73-0.75），优于输出置信度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性top-k KV缓存驱逐无法感知被丢弃的内容，导致注意力输出误差可能任意大且无法被服务时的误差估计器一致估计，需要可认证的误差估计方法。

Method: 提出随机化驱逐方案：对尾部进行泊松采样（已知包含概率），在softmax内使用单个logit偏移实现哈耶克校正，并基于调查抽样方差估计对保留集计算每步误差证书。在真实负载上预注册七个假设进行验证。

Result: 随机化证书在经验覆盖率达0.97且无精度损失。七个预注册声明中三个未通过：问题感知驱逐在25-50%预算下几乎无开销；输出对数概率预测失败优于证书；证书门控的预算递增无效果。成功的是归因：证书区分缓存导致与固有失败的AUC为0.73-0.75，优于输出置信度的0.47-0.54，并可指导重计算调度。

Conclusion: 随机化提升的是归因能力（attribution）而非预测能力，确定性驱逐本质不可知，随机化恢复可识别性但代价是预测失败能力有限。

Abstract: Deterministic KV-cache eviction keeps the top-$k$ tokens under an importance score and deletes the rest. We prove that this design cannot know what it destroyed: evicted values can be altered so that everything the serving system retains is unchanged while the true attention-output error grows arbitrarily, so no serving-time estimator of that error is consistent. Randomized eviction restores identifiability. With a Poisson-sampled tail at known inclusion probabilities, one logit offset performs the Hájek correction inside the softmax, and a survey-sampling variance estimator over the retained set becomes a per-step error certificate with 0.97 empirical coverage at no accuracy cost. On real workloads we pre-registered seven claims and lost three: question-aware eviction at 25--50\% budgets is nearly free; output log-probability predicts failure better than the certificate; certificate-gated budget escalation adds nothing. What survives is attribution: the certificate separates cache-induced from inherent failures (AUC 0.73--0.75, against 0.47--0.54 for output confidence) and schedules recomputation better than random or confidence gating. Randomization buys attribution, not prediction.

</details>


### [205] [Compact Latent Coordination for Autonomous Vehicles at Unsignalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21488)
*Gil Lifshits,Igal Bilik,Gilad Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出主智体-原型规划系统（MAPS），一种分层深度强化学习架构，通过集中式主智体生成全局协调嵌入（proto-plan），分散式工作智体结合局部观测执行车辆控制，在无信号交叉口实现无碰撞导航并显著降低平均通行时间，且具备零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无信号交叉口的多智能体协调是多智能体强化学习系统的关键挑战，现有方法面临组合动作空间、依赖特权信息或智能体设计僵化等问题。

Method: 提出MAPS分层DRL架构：集中式Master智能体生成紧凑连续嵌入（proto-plan）编码全局协调策略，分散式Worker智能体融合该嵌入与局部观测执行车辆控制，实现策略意图与战术执行的解耦及各模块独立优化。

Result: 在72种交叉口配置中实现无碰撞导航，显著降低平均通行时间，超越最先进基线；三智能体训练的模型零样本部署至五智能体场景时成功率达94%。

Conclusion: 基于proto-plan的分层学习为多车协调提供了有前景的框架，证明了该机制在泛化性和性能上的优势。

Abstract: Coordinating autonomous vehicles at unsignalized intersections remains a critical challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems, which typically struggle with combinatorial action spaces, reliance on privileged information, or rigid agent designs. We propose Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS), a hierarchical deep reinforcement learning (DRL) architecture in which a centralized Master agent generates a compact, continuous embedding, denoted as proto-plan, that encodes a global coordination strategy. Decentralized Worker agents integrate this embedding with local observations to execute vehicle-specific control, decoupling strategic intent from tactical execution and enabling independent optimization of each module.
  As a proof-of-concept evaluation of this coordination mechanism, we test MAPS across 72 intersection configurations in HighwayEnv. MAPS achieves collision-free navigation while significantly reducing average travel time, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. The learned proto-plans further exhibit robust generalization: a system trained with three agents achieves a 94% success rate when deployed zero-shot to five-agent scenarios, confirming that proto-plan-based hierarchical learning provides a promising framework for multi-vehicle coordination.

</details>


### [206] [Windowed-MTP: Removing the Full-Context Draft-KV Tax at Million-Token Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21535)
*Alagappan Valliappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长上下文场景下原生MTP草稿头因全注意力KV读取成本过高导致推测解码效率下降的问题，提出Windowed-MTP方法：在草稿注意力上施加滑动窗口+注意力汇聚，将KV工作集约束为常数，丢弃约99%的KV条目，同时保持目标模型的全注意力验证不变。该方法无需训练、即插即用且无损，在1M上下文、单GPU测试中，解码步成本降低28%-44%，端到端延迟同比例改善，并可通过环形缓冲区回收未读取的草稿KV（约占7.7-11%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码假设草稿模型足够廉价，但在百万token上下文下，MTP草稿头每步需对整个KV缓存执行全注意力，其读取成本随上下文线性增长，成为主导开销，使得推测解码在最需要加速的长上下文场景下效率反而下降，甚至变慢于无推测解码。

Method: 仅在草稿注意力上应用StreamingLLM风格的滑动窗口加注意力汇聚（Windowed-MTP），将草稿的KV工作集约束为常数（丢弃约99%的KV条目），而目标模型的全注意力验证保持不动。草稿仅提出候选token，最终接受决策仍由全注意力目标模型做出，因此该方法理论上无损。同时，未被读取的草稿KV通过紧凑环形缓冲区回收。

Result: 在三个架构族（Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B、Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B）上，1M上下文单GPU测试：相比原生MTP草稿，每解码步成本降低28%-44%，该优势与输入无关且随上下文增长而扩大。在匹配接受率下端到端延迟同比例改善，若窗口化还提升接受率则改善更大。回收未读草稿KV占总量7.7-11%，且不损失接受率或质量。

Conclusion: Windowed-MTP通过将草稿注意力限制为常数窗口，在不影响目标模型验证质量的前提下，有效解决了长上下文场景下MTP草稿头计算成本过高的问题，是一种训练免费、即插即用且无损的优化方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by having a cheap draft propose tokens that a target verifies in parallel. Frontier models increasingly ship a built-in Multi-Token-Prediction (MTP/NEXTN) draft head under the assumption that the draft is negligibly cheap. At million-token context this breaks: an MTP draft head typically runs full attention over the entire KV cache at every draft step, so its read grows linearly with context and comes to dominate the draft cost -- precisely where speculation is most valuable. The effect compounds with draft length (a deep native draft can turn net-negative, slower than no speculation) and sharpens under hybrid/linear-attention targets, where cheaper verification leaves the draft's full-attention read exposed. We apply a StreamingLLM-style sliding window plus attention sink to the draft's attention only (Windowed-MTP), leaving full-attention verification intact. It is training-free, drop-in, and lossless by construction: the full-attention target still decides every accepted token, so windowing changes only which tokens are proposed, never which are accepted. It bounds the draft's KV working set to a constant, dropping ~99% of KV entries at 1M. Across three architecture families (Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B and a Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B) at 1M context on a single GPU in SGLang, windowing cuts the per-decode-step cost over the shipping native MTP draft by +28% to +44%, an input-invariant margin that widens with context. Since per-token latency is this cost divided by acceptance length, at matched acceptance end-to-end decode latency improves by the same amount, and more where windowing also lifts acceptance, while preserving the target's verified output distribution. Finally, the unread draft KV -- 7.7-11% of total KV at 1M -- is reclaimed via a compact ring buffer at no acceptance or quality cost.

</details>


### [207] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出X$^3$-OPD，一种跨模态在策略蒸馏框架，将文本教师模型的推理能力迁移至音频-语言学生模型，通过三层对称语料库（文本推理转语音、音频事件推理、口语对话推理）改善音频基础推理和思维链质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频-语言模型在深度逻辑推理方面落后于文本大语言模型，主要原因是高质量音频推理数据的稀缺。

Method: 提出X$^3$-OPD跨模态在策略蒸馏框架：学生模型基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹，教师模型使用匹配文本输入和验证答案提供token级指导。同时构建三层对称语料库，覆盖文本推理转语音、音频事件推理和口语对话推理。

Result: 在MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio和MMAR基准上，X$^3$-OPD显著提升了音频基础推理和思维链质量，同时在域迁移下保持了模型现有能力。

Conclusion: X$^3$-OPD通过跨模态蒸馏有效弥合了音频-语言模型与文本模型之间的推理差距，并将蒸馏扩展至非语言事件、韵律和对话语境等不可文本恢复的推理内容。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>


### [208] [Graph Learning on Ensembles of Cyclic Peptides: An Investigation of Molecular Ensemble Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21561)
*Aaron Feller,Kris Deibler,Maxim Secor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EnsembleEGNN，一种分子构象集成基础模型，通过共享EGNN层编码每个构象，再用Set Attention Block池化，在环肽数据集CREMP上预训练。预训练模型在CREMP-CycPeptMPDB上R²=0.477，优于序列BERT基线；与BERT联合训练进一步提升至R²=0.538，证明编码构象集成可提升环肽性质预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测通常仅使用单一代表性构象，但许多分子在溶液中以构象集成形式存在，忽略构象多样性可能导致预测不准确。因此需要一种能编码完整构象集成的模型。

Method: 提出EnsembleEGNN模型：首先用共享的等变图神经网络（EGNN）层分别编码每个构象，然后用Set Attention Block将所有构象表示池化为单一嵌入。在CREMP（环肽构象集成数据集）上使用多任务自监督预训练，包括掩码token恢复、噪声坐标重建和成对距离重建。此外还探索了与BERT序列编码器的联合训练。

Result: 从零训练EnsembleEGNN完全失败（R²=0.005）；预训练模型达到R²=0.477、Pearson r=0.699，优于序列BERT基线（R²=0.439, r=0.667）；与BERT联合训练的混合模型进一步提升至R²=0.538、Pearson r=0.737。

Conclusion: 将构象集成编码为单一热力学信息的嵌入能显著提升环肽性质预测性能，验证了构象多样性信息对分子性质预测的重要性。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction from structure often uses a single representative conformation, even though many molecules exist as conformational ensembles in solution. We introduce EnsembleEGNN, a molecular ensemble foundation model that encodes an ensemble by first encoding each conformer with shared Equivariant Graph Neural Network (EGNN) layers, then pooling the resulting conformer representations with a Set Attention Block. We pretrain the model on CREMP, a cyclic peptide ensemble dataset, using a multi-task self-supervised objective combining masked token recovery, noisy-coordinate reconstruction, and pairwise distance reconstruction. On the CREMP-CycPeptMPDB dataset, training EnsembleEGNN from scratch fails entirely ($R^2=0.005$). However, the pretrained model reaches $R^2=0.477$ and Pearson $r=0.699$, outperforming the sequence-only BERT baseline ($R^2=0.439$, Pearson $r=0.667$). When EnsembleEGNN is co-trained end-to-end with the BERT sequence encoder, the hybrid model improves further to $R^2=0.538$ and Pearson $r=0.737$. These results demonstrate that encoding conformational ensembles into a single thermodynamically informed embedding improves cyclic-peptide property prediction.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Sufficiency: Time Series Explanation with Counterfactual Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21573)
*Hongnan Ma,Yiwei Shi,Mengyue Yang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimePNS是一个面向时间序列分类器解释的必要性感知框架，采用两阶段设计：第一阶段学习可识别的因果生成过程和充分性解释掩码，第二阶段通过反事实干预生成必要性信号，优化初始解释以突出必要子序列，在合成和真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列可解释性方法主要关注充分性（sufficiency），可能将支持预测但非关键的子序列误判为重要，缺乏必要性（necessity）考量，无法区分真正决策关键的子序列与伪相关特征。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage I学习可识别的因果生成过程与充分性解释掩码；Stage II基于Pearl反事实必要性概念，通过干预时间因素生成必要性信号，监督时间门控机制筛选初始解释，抑制非必要成分、强调反事实必要的子序列。

Result: 在合成和真实世界时间序列基准上，TimePNS能更准确识别决策关键子序列，在充分性-必要性权衡上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 将反事实必要性引入时间序列解释有效弥补了纯充分性方法的不足，必要性约束能剔除伪相关特征，提升解释的精确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Faithful explanations of time-series classifiers should identify subsequences that are not only sufficient to preserve a black-box model's prediction, but also necessary for maintaining it. However, existing sufficiency-oriented methods can assign high importance to spurious subsequences that support the prediction without being essential to the model's decision. We introduce \textbf{TimePNS}, a necessity-aware framework for time-series explanation. Inspired by Pearl's counterfactual notion of necessity, TimePNS assesses whether a temporal factor is necessary by intervening on it and measuring whether the original prediction is disrupted. The framework adopts a two-stage design. Stage I learns an identifiable causal generative process together with a sufficiency-oriented explanation mask. Stage II performs counterfactual interventions on temporal factors to derive necessity signals, which supervise a temporal gate that refines the initial explanation by suppressing non-essential components and emphasizing counterfactually necessary ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that TimePNS more accurately identifies decision-critical subsequences and consistently improves sufficiency-necessity trade-offs over strong baselines.

</details>


### [210] [Expanding Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21585)
*Sophia Tang,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出扩展生成流(EFlows)和扩展流映射(EFMs)，通过递增维度插值实现变尺寸生成，适用于连续和离散状态空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的生成模型受限于固定维度或固定序列长度，无法处理输出大小可变的生成任务。

Method: 定义扩展插值器通过条件噪声递增维度；EFM分解为扩展算子(增广状态空间)和传输映射(沿插值推进)，两者组合实现联合扩展与去噪。

Result: 在连续和离散模态(包括变尺寸图生成和变长序列生成)上验证了框架有效性，固定画布流和流映射是EFlows/EFMs的特例。

Conclusion: EFlows和EFMs为输出大小本身作为可学习、可控制自由度的场景提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have enabled remarkable progress in fast and controllable generation across continuous and discrete state spaces, yet existing parameterizations are constrained to fixed dimensions or fixed sequence lengths. Here, we introduce Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows), which define flows between distributions of increasing dimensionality along an expanding interpolant that grows the state by augmenting it with conditional noise. Building on this construction, we propose Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs), a new class of flow maps that distill the expanding interpolant into efficient few-step generative models. Each EFM factors the map between any two timesteps into two learnable operations: an expand operator, which augments the state space with new coordinates or tokens conditioned on the current state, and a transport map, which pushes the expanded state forward along the interpolant. Composing these operators yields a single map that jointly expands and denoises the state, recovering existing fixed-canvas flows and flow maps as the special case in which the expand operator is the identity. We further extend the framework to the discrete simplex, enabling variable-size graph generation and variable-length sequence generation. Across both continuous and discrete modalities, we establish EFlows and EFMs as a principled framework for settings in which output size is itself a learned, controllable degree of freedom.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [AINTMA: Agentic AI Architecture for Autonomous Test Management with Generative Intelligence, Secure Cloud Communication and Adaptive Quality Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20452)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Shyalendar Reddy Allala,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi,Srinivasateja Songa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AINTMA是一个基于多智能体代理AI的自主测试管理架构，通过六个专用AI代理（测试发现、风险评估、强化学习优先级排序、执行编排、生成式质量智能、云安全监控）在云原生微服务基础设施上协同工作，显著提升测试效率与质量。在12个异构软件项目、18个月的评估中，实现了88.4%的测试优先级排序准确率、43%的测试周期缩短、缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%，以及340%的投资回报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件质量保证需要能够在分布式云环境中自适应决策的智能自主系统，传统测试管理方法无法满足这一需求，亟需向自主质量智能生态系统转型。

Method: 提出AINTMA，包含六个专门化AI代理（测试发现、风险评估、RL优先级排序、执行编排、生成式质量智能、云安全监控），通过安全的多代理通信框架在云原生微服务基础设施上协调运行。RL优先级排序代理将测试选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，从大规模历史测试执行数据（47个特征、36个月滚动窗口）中学习上下文策略；生成式质量智能代理利用大语言模型生成自然语言质量报告；安全通信通过零信任API网关、OAuth2/JWT认证、加密代理间消息和多租户隔离实现。

Result: 88.4%测试优先级排序准确率（APFD，对比随机51.2%、最佳商业基线82.1%）；测试周期缩短43%；缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%；340% ROI，9个月回本；可扩展至50,000+测试用例且响应时间低于400ms；生成式智能模块开发者有用性评分4.3/5.0。

Conclusion: 结合自主多代理协同、生成式智能和安全的智能连接，代理型AI能够从根本上推进云规模企业环境中的软件质量管理。

Abstract: Modern software quality assurance demands intelligent, autonomous systems capable of adaptive decision-making across distributed cloud environments. This paper presents AINTMA (Agentic Intelligent Test Management Architecture), a multi-agent agentic AI system that transforms traditional test management into an autonomous quality intelligence ecosystem. AINTMA deploys six specialized AI agents (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, Reinforcement Learning Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, and Cloud Security Monitor) coordinated through a secure multi-agent communication framework over a cloud-native microservices infrastructure. The Generative Quality Intelligence agent employs large language models to produce plain language quality narratives, defect risk summaries, and data-augmented test recommendations. The RL Prioritization agent models test selection as a Markov Decision Process, learning contextual policies from large-scale historical test execution data (47 features, rolling 36-month window). Secure cloud communication is enforced through a zero-trust API gateway with OAuth2/JWT authentication, encrypted inter-agent messaging, and multi-tenant isolation. Evaluation across 12 heterogeneous software projects over 18 months demonstrates: 88.4% test prioritization accuracy (APFD, vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline); 43% test cycle time reduction; defect escape rate reduced from 8.3% to 2.1%; 340% ROI at 9-month payback. The agentic architecture scales to 50,000+ test cases with sub-400ms response time, and the generative intelligence module achieves 4.3/5.0 developer usefulness rating. AINTMA demonstrates that agentic AI, combining autonomous multi-agent coordination, generative intelligence and secure smart connectivity, can fundamentally advance software quality management in cloud-scale enterprise environments.

</details>


### [212] [ConfidenceBench: Evaluating Confidence Calibration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20526)
*Matthew ffrench-Constant,Daniel Yang,Xinmeng Huang,Sanyam Kapoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ConfidenceBench基准，评估15个前沿LLM的言语化置信度校准，发现准确率与校准度之间存在显著差异，最准确的模型并非校准最佳的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在需要高可靠性的场景中部署时，流畅但错误的回答可能带来高昂成本，因此模型需要知道自己何时可能出错，而传统仅基于准确率的评估不足以衡量模型可靠性。

Method: 构建包含200个私有多项选择题的基准测试，涵盖空间推理、高精度数学、单词查找和不可知问题四类；通过提示方式 eliciting 言语化置信度估计，使用Brier分数评估15个前沿LLM的校准性能；无需访问模型logits，适用于闭源和开源系统。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6和Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview取得最低Brier分数（0.103），远优于校准随机基线（0.1875）；Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite得分0.367，校准严重不佳；准确率与校准度在不同模型家族间显著分离。

Conclusion: 言语化置信度校准是LLM可靠性的一个独立且重要的维度，与基于准确率的评估互为补充。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings where fluent but incorrect answers can be costly. In these settings, accuracy alone is insufficient: models must also know when they are likely to be wrong. We present ConfidenceBench, a calibration benchmark that evaluates verbalized confidence estimates in 15 frontier LLMs using the Brier score, a proper scoring rule that incentivises truthful probability reporting.
